   * Create and start a tracer.

Method code:

235 |     this.type = type;
236 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;

238 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
239 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
240 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
241 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
242 |       int i = 0;
243 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
244 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
245 |         i++;
246 |       }
247 |     }

250 | 
251 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
252 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
253 |       return;

257 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
258 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
259 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
260 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
261 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
262 |       trace.truncateEvents();
263 |     }
264 | 
265 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
266 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
267 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,

271 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
272 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));

274 |     }

===== M0003 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getStringNumberValue(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 312-353
Documentation:

Method code:
312 |   static Double getStringNumberValue(String rawJsString) {
313 |       // vertical tab is not always whitespace
314 | 
315 |     String s = trimJsWhiteSpace(rawJsString);
316 |     // return ScriptRuntime.toNumber(s);
317 |     if (s.length() == 0) {
318 |       return 0.0;
319 |     }
320 | 
321 |     if (s.length() > 2
322 |         && s.charAt(0) == '0'
323 |         && (s.charAt(1) == 'x' || s.charAt(1) == 'X')) {
324 |       // Attempt to convert hex numbers.
325 |       try {
326 |         return Double.valueOf(Integer.parseInt(s.substring(2), 16));
327 |       } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
328 |         return Double.NaN;
329 |       }
330 |     }
331 | 
332 |     if (s.length() > 3
333 |         && (s.charAt(0) == '-' || s.charAt(0) == '+')
334 |         && s.charAt(1) == '0'
335 |         && (s.charAt(2) == 'x' || s.charAt(2) == 'X')) {
336 |       // hex numbers with explicit signs vary between browsers.
337 |       return null;
338 |     }
339 | 
340 |     // FireFox and IE treat the "Infinity" differently. FireFox is case
341 |     // insensitive, but IE treats "infinity" as NaN.  So leave it alone.
342 |     if (s.equals("infinity")
343 |         || s.equals("-infinity")
344 |         || s.equals("+infinity")) {
345 |       return null;
346 |     }
347 | 
348 |     try {
349 |       return Double.parseDouble(s);
350 |     } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
351 |       return Double.NaN;
352 |     }
353 |   }

===== M0011 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 923-1041
Documentation:

Method code:
923 |   private void toString(
924 |       StringBuilder sb,
925 |       boolean printSource,
926 |       boolean printAnnotations,
927 |       boolean printType) {
928 |     if (Token.printTrees) {
929 |       sb.append(Token.name(type));
930 |       if (this instanceof StringNode) {
931 |         sb.append(' ');
932 |         sb.append(getString());
933 |       } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
934 |         sb.append(' ');
935 |         // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
936 |         // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
937 |         // toStringTree.
938 |         if (first == null || first.getType() != Token.NAME) {
939 |           sb.append("<invalid>");
940 |         } else {
941 |           sb.append(first.getString());
942 |         }
943 |       } else if (this instanceof ScriptOrFnNode) {
944 |         ScriptOrFnNode sof = (ScriptOrFnNode) this;
945 |         if (this instanceof FunctionNode) {
946 |           FunctionNode fn = (FunctionNode) this;
947 |           sb.append(' ');
948 |           sb.append(fn.getFunctionName());
949 |         }
950 |         if (printSource) {
951 |           sb.append(" [source name: ");
952 |           sb.append(sof.getSourceName());
953 |           sb.append("] [encoded source length: ");
954 |           sb.append(sof.getEncodedSourceEnd() - sof.getEncodedSourceStart());
955 |           sb.append("] [base line: ");
956 |           sb.append(sof.getBaseLineno());
957 |           sb.append("] [end line: ");
958 |           sb.append(sof.getEndLineno());
959 |           sb.append(']');
960 |         }
961 |       } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
962 |         sb.append(' ');
963 |         sb.append(getDouble());
964 |       }
965 |       if (printSource) {
966 |         int lineno = getLineno();
967 |         if (lineno != -1) {
968 |           sb.append(' ');
969 |           sb.append(lineno);
970 |         }
971 |       }
972 | 
973 |       if (printAnnotations) {
974 |         int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
975 |         for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
976 |           int type = keys[i];
977 |           PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
978 |           sb.append(" [");
979 |           sb.append(propToString(type));
980 |           sb.append(": ");
981 |           String value;
982 |           switch (type) {
983 |             case TARGETBLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it recurses
984 |               value = "target block property";
985 |               break;
986 |             case LOCAL_BLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it is dull
987 |               value = "last local block";
988 |               break;
989 |             case ISNUMBER_PROP:
990 |               switch (x.intValue) {
991 |                 case BOTH:
992 |                   value = "both";
993 |                   break;
994 |                 case RIGHT:
995 |                   value = "right";
996 |                   break;
997 |                 case LEFT:
998 |                   value = "left";
999 |                   break;
1000 |                 default:
1001 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1002 |               }
1003 |               break;
1004 |             case SPECIALCALL_PROP:
1005 |               switch (x.intValue) {
1006 |                 case SPECIALCALL_EVAL:
1007 |                   value = "eval";
1008 |                   break;
1009 |                 case SPECIALCALL_WITH:
1010 |                   value = "with";
1011 |                   break;
1012 |                 default:
1013 |                   // NON_SPECIALCALL should not be stored
1014 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1015 |               }
1016 |               break;
1017 |             default:
1018 |               Object obj = x.objectValue;
1019 |               if (obj != null) {
1020 |                 value = obj.toString();
1021 |               } else {
1022 |                 value = String.valueOf(x.intValue);
1023 |               }
1024 |               break;
1025 |           }
1026 |           sb.append(value);
1027 |           sb.append(']');
1028 |         }
1029 |       }
1030 | 
1031 |       if (printType) {
1032 |         if (jsType != null) {
1033 |           String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
1034 |           if (jsTypeString != null) {
1035 |             sb.append(" : ");
1036 |             sb.append(jsTypeString);
1037 |           }
1038 |         }
1039 |       }
1040 |     }
1041 |   }

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1158 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1159 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1160 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1161 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1162 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1163 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1164 |       } else {
1165 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1166 |       }
1167 |     }
1168 |     return builder.build();
1169 |   }

Method code:
1206 |   static boolean isNumericResultHelper(Node n) {
1207 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1208 |       case Token.ADD:
1209 |         return !mayBeString(n.getFirstChild())
1210 |             && !mayBeString(n.getLastChild());
1211 |       case Token.BITNOT:
1212 |       case Token.BITOR:
1213 |       case Token.BITXOR:
1214 |       case Token.BITAND:
1215 |       case Token.LSH:
1216 |       case Token.RSH:
1217 |       case Token.URSH:
1218 |       case Token.SUB:
1219 |       case Token.MUL:
1220 |       case Token.MOD:
1221 |       case Token.DIV:
1222 |       case Token.INC:
1223 |       case Token.DEC:
1224 |       case Token.POS:
1225 |       case Token.NEG:
1226 |       case Token.NUMBER:
1227 |         return true;
1228 |       case Token.NAME:
1229 |         String name = n.getString();
1230 |         if (name.equals("NaN")) {
1231 |           return true;
1232 |         }
1233 |         if (name.equals("Infinity")) {
1234 |           return true;
1235 |         }
1236 |         return false;
1237 |       default:
1238 |         return false;
1239 |     }
1240 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.FunctionType(JSTypeRegistry,String,Node,ArrowType,ObjectType,String,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java

Documentation:
/** Creates an instance for a function that might be a constructor. */
Method code:
122 |   FunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name, Node source,
123 |       ArrowType arrowType, ObjectType typeOfThis,
124 |       String templateTypeName,  boolean isConstructor, boolean nativeType) {
125 |     super(registry, name,
126 |         registry.getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_INSTANCE_TYPE),
127 |         nativeType);
128 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(source == null ||
129 |         Token.FUNCTION == source.getType());

136 |     } else {

621 |   private void addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
622 |       String propertyName, JSType type) {
623 |     if (type instanceof ObjectType && ((ObjectType) type).hasReferenceName()) {
624 |       Map<String, ObjectType> typeSet =
625 |           eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ErrorFunctionType.ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry,String)

Method code:
50 |   ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name) {
51 |     super(
52 |         registry, name, null,

54 |             registry.createOptionalParameters(
55 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeOptimizationsPass.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)

Documentation:

Method code:
61 |   @Override
62 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
63 |     Node currentVersionOfNode = n;
64 |     boolean somethingChanged = false;
65 | 
66 |     do {
67 |       somethingChanged = false;
68 |       for (AbstractPeepholeOptimization optimization : peepholeOptimizations) {
69 |         Node newVersionOfNode =
70 |             optimization.optimizeSubtree(currentVersionOfNode);
71 | 
72 |         if (newVersionOfNode != currentVersionOfNode) {
73 |           somethingChanged = true;
74 | 
75 |           currentVersionOfNode = newVersionOfNode;
76 |         }
77 | 
78 |         if (currentVersionOfNode == null) {
79 |           return;
80 |         }
81 |       }
82 |     } while(somethingChanged);
83 |   }

===== M0055 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 422-466
Documentation:

Method code:
422 |   @Override
423 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
424 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
425 |       return true;
426 |     }
427 | 
428 |     // Union types
429 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
430 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
431 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
432 |       return false;
433 |     }
434 | 
435 |     // record types
436 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
437 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
438 |     }
439 | 
440 |     // Interfaces
441 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
442 |     // to the interface instance.
443 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
444 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
445 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
446 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
447 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
448 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
449 |           return true;
450 |         }
451 |       }
452 |     }
453 | 
454 |     // other prototype based objects
455 |     if (that != null) {
456 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
457 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
458 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
459 |         // to avoid guessing.
460 |         return true;
461 |       }
462 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
463 |     }
464 | 
465 |     return false;
466 |   }

===== M0056 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 437-465
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
437 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
438 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
439 |     int type = n.getType();
440 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
441 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     curNode = n;
445 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
446 | 
447 |     switch (type) {
448 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
449 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
450 |         break;
451 | 
452 |       default:
453 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
454 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
455 |           // would no longer point to the true next
456 |           Node next = child.getNext();
457 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
458 |           child = next;
459 |         }
460 |         break;
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     curNode = n;
464 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
465 |   }

===== M0074 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeChild(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 680-689
Documentation:
/**
   * Detach a child from its parent and siblings.
   */
Method code:
680 |   public void removeChild(Node child) {
681 |     Node prev = getChildBefore(child);
682 |     if (prev == null)
683 |         first = first.next;
684 |     else
685 |         prev.next = child.next;
686 |     if (child == last) last = prev;
687 |     child.next = null;
688 |     child.parent = null;
689 |   }

116 |   private IRFactory(String sourceString,
117 |                     String sourceName,
118 |                     Config config,
119 |                     ErrorReporter errorReporter) {
120 |     this.sourceString = sourceString;
121 |     this.sourceName = sourceName;
122 |     this.config = config;
123 |     this.errorReporter = errorReporter;
124 |     this.transformDispatcher = new TransformDispatcher();

126 |     this.templateNode = createTemplateNode();
127 | 
128 |     switch (config.languageMode) {

130 |         // Reserved words are handled by the Rhino parser.
131 |         reservedKeywords = null;

===== M0106 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 467-481
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
467 |   @Override
468 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
469 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
470 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
471 |     if (unknown) {
472 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
473 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
474 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
475 |         unknown = false;
476 |       } else {
477 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
478 |       }
479 |     }
480 |     return unknown;
481 |   }

===== M0108 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 221-279
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
221 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
222 |     this.options = options;
223 |     if (errorManager == null) {
224 |       if (outStream == null) {
225 |         setErrorManager(
226 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
227 |       } else {
228 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
229 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
230 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
231 |         setErrorManager(printer);
232 |       }
233 |     }
234 | 
235 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
236 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
237 |       options.checkTypes = true;
238 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
239 |       options.checkTypes = false;
240 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
241 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
242 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
243 |       // parser type warnings.
244 |       options.setWarningLevel(
245 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
246 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
247 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn()) {
251 |       options.setWarningLevel(
252 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
253 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
254 |     }
255 | 
256 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
257 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
258 |     guards.add(
259 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
260 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
261 |     WarningsGuard warningsGuard = options.getWarningsGuard();
262 |     if (warningsGuard != null) {
263 |       guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
267 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
268 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
269 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
270 |     // error off.
271 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
272 |         (warningsGuard == null || !warningsGuard.disables(
273 |             DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES))) {
274 |       guards.add(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
275 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
276 |     }
277 | 
278 |     this.warningsGuard = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
279 |   }

Original range: 306-314
Documentation:

Method code:
306 |   private int position2charno(int position) {
307 |     int lineIndex = sourceString.lastIndexOf('\n', position);
308 |     if (lineIndex == -1) {
309 |       return position;
310 |     } else {
311 |       // Subtract one for initial position being 0.
312 |       return position - lineIndex - 1;
313 |     }
314 |   }

===== M0120 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 775-789
Documentation:
/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
775 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
776 |     if (item == null) {
777 |       return null;
778 |     } else if (item.type == propType) {
779 |       return item.next;
780 |     } else {
781 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.next, propType);
782 |       if (result != item.next) {
783 |         return new PropListItem(
784 |             item.type, item.intValue, item.objectValue, result);
785 |       } else {
786 |         return item;
787 |       }
788 |     }
789 |   }

Original range: 632-658
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
632 |   @Override
633 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType otherType) {
634 |     if (!(otherType instanceof FunctionType)) {
635 |       return false;
636 |     }
637 |     FunctionType that = (FunctionType) otherType;
638 |     if (!that.isFunctionType()) {
639 |       return false;
640 |     }
641 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
642 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
643 |         return this == that;
644 |       }
645 |       return false;
646 |     }
647 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
648 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
649 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
650 |       }
651 |       return false;
652 |     }
653 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
654 |       return false;
655 |     }
656 |     return this.typeOfThis.isEquivalentTo(that.typeOfThis) &&
657 |         this.call.isEquivalentTo(that.call);
658 |   }

===== M0139 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getPureBooleanValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 117-157
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the boolean value of a node that represents a literal. This method
   * effectively emulates the <code>Boolean()</code> JavaScript cast function
   * except it return UNKNOWN for known values with side-effects, use
   * getExpressionBooleanValue if you don't care about side-effects.
   */
Method code:
117 |   static TernaryValue getPureBooleanValue(Node n) {
118 |     switch (n.getType()) {
119 |       case Token.STRING:
120 |         return TernaryValue.forBoolean(n.getString().length() > 0);
121 | 
122 |       case Token.NUMBER:
123 |         return TernaryValue.forBoolean(n.getDouble() != 0);
124 | 
125 |       case Token.NOT:
126 |         return getPureBooleanValue(n.getLastChild()).not();
127 | 
128 |       case Token.NULL:
129 |       case Token.FALSE:
130 |       case Token.VOID:
131 |         return TernaryValue.FALSE;
132 | 
133 |       case Token.NAME:
134 |         String name = n.getString();
135 |         if ("undefined".equals(name)
136 |             || "NaN".equals(name)) {
137 |           // We assume here that programs don't change the value of the keyword
138 |           // undefined to something other than the value undefined.
139 |           return TernaryValue.FALSE;
140 |         } else if ("Infinity".equals(name)) {
141 |           return TernaryValue.TRUE;
142 |         }
143 |         break;
144 | 
145 |       case Token.TRUE:
146 |       case Token.REGEXP:
147 |         return TernaryValue.TRUE;
148 | 
149 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
150 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
151 |         if (!mayHaveSideEffects(n)) {
152 |           return TernaryValue.TRUE;
153 |         }
154 |     }
155 | 
156 |     return TernaryValue.UNKNOWN;
157 |   }

===== M0142 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1148-1156
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1148 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1149 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1150 |       return -1;
1151 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1152 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1153 |     } else {
1154 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1155 |     }
1156 |   }

===== M0143 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.cloneTree()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1877-1891
Documentation:
/**
   * @return A detached clone of the Node and all its children.
   */
Method code:
1877 |   public Node cloneTree() {
1878 |     Node result = cloneNode();
1879 |     for (Node n2 = getFirstChild(); n2 != null; n2 = n2.getNext()) {
1880 |       Node n2clone = n2.cloneTree();
1881 |       n2clone.parent = result;
1882 |       if (result.last != null) {
1883 |         result.last.next = n2clone;
1884 |       }
1885 |       if (result.first == null) {
1886 |         result.first = n2clone;
1887 |       }
1888 |       result.last = n2clone;
1889 |     }
1890 |     return result;
1891 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1093-1167
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
1093 |   static int precedence(int type) {
1094 |     switch (type) {
1095 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
1096 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
1097 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
1098 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
1099 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
1100 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
1101 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
1102 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
1103 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
1104 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
1105 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
1106 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
1107 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
1108 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
1109 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
1110 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
1111 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
1112 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
1113 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
1114 |       case Token.EQ:
1115 |       case Token.NE:
1116 |       case Token.SHEQ:
1117 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
1118 |       case Token.LT:
1119 |       case Token.GT:
1120 |       case Token.LE:
1121 |       case Token.GE:
1122 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
1123 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
1124 |       case Token.LSH:
1125 |       case Token.RSH:
1126 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
1127 |       case Token.SUB:
1128 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
1129 |       case Token.MUL:
1130 |       case Token.MOD:
1131 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
1132 |       case Token.INC:
1133 |       case Token.DEC:
1134 |       case Token.NEW:
1135 |       case Token.DELPROP:
1136 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
1137 |       case Token.VOID:
1138 |       case Token.NOT:
1139 |       case Token.BITNOT:
1140 |       case Token.POS:
1141 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
1142 | 
1143 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
1144 |       case Token.CALL:
1145 |       case Token.EMPTY:
1146 |       case Token.FALSE:
1147 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1148 |       case Token.GETELEM:
1149 |       case Token.GETPROP:
1150 |       case Token.GET_REF:
1151 |       case Token.IF:
1152 |       case Token.LP:
1153 |       case Token.NAME:
1154 |       case Token.NULL:
1155 |       case Token.NUMBER:
1156 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
1157 |       case Token.REGEXP:
1158 |       case Token.STRING:
1159 |       case Token.THIS:
1160 |       case Token.TRUE:
1161 |         return 15;
1162 | 
1163 |       default: throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
1164 |                                Node.tokenToName(type) +
1165 |                                " (type " + type + ")");
1166 |     }
1167 |   }

===== M0152 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(int,Appendable)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1142-1160
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns a javascript representation of the character in a hex escaped
   * format.
   *
   * @param codePoint The codepoint to append.
   * @param out The buffer to which the hex representation should be appended.
   */
Method code:
1142 |   private static void appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(
1143 |       int codePoint, Appendable out)
1144 |       throws IOException {
1145 |     if (Character.isSupplementaryCodePoint(codePoint)) {
1146 |       // Handle supplementary unicode values which are not representable in
1147 |       // javascript.  We deal with these by escaping them as two 4B sequences
1148 |       // so that they will round-trip properly when sent from java to javascript
1149 |       // and back.
1150 |       char[] surrogates = Character.toChars(codePoint);
1151 |       appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(surrogates[0], out);
1152 |       appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(surrogates[1], out);
1153 |       return;
1154 |     }
1155 |     out.append("\\u")
1156 |         .append(HEX_CHARS[(codePoint >>> 12) & 0xf])
1157 |         .append(HEX_CHARS[(codePoint >>> 8) & 0xf])
1158 |         .append(HEX_CHARS[(codePoint >>> 4) & 0xf])
1159 |         .append(HEX_CHARS[codePoint & 0xf]);
1160 |   }

===== M0154 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 82-720
Documentation:

Method code:
82 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
83 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
84 |       return;
85 |     }
86 | 
87 |     int type = n.getType();
88 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
89 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
90 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
91 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
92 | 
93 |     // Handle all binary operators
94 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
95 |       Preconditions.checkState(
96 |           childCount == 2,
97 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
98 |           opstr, childCount);
99 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
100 |       addLeftExpr(first, p, context);
101 |       cc.addOp(opstr, true);
102 | 
103 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
104 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
105 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
106 | 
107 |       // Handle associativity.
108 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
109 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
110 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
111 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
112 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
113 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
114 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
115 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
116 |       } else {
117 |         addExpr(last, p + 1, rhsContext);
118 |       }
119 |       return;
120 |     }
121 | 
122 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
123 | 
124 |     switch (type) {
125 |       case Token.TRY: {
126 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().getType() == Token.BLOCK &&
127 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
128 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
129 | 
130 |         add("try");
131 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
132 | 
133 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
134 |         // isn't a catch block
135 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
136 |         if (catchblock != null) {
137 |           add(catchblock);
138 |         }
139 | 
140 |         if (childCount == 3) {
141 |           add("finally");
142 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
143 |         }
144 |         break;
145 |       }
146 | 
147 |       case Token.CATCH:
148 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
149 |         add("catch(");
150 |         add(first);
151 |         add(")");
152 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
153 |         break;
154 | 
155 |       case Token.THROW:
156 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
157 |         add("throw");
158 |         add(first);
159 | 
160 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
161 |         // parse this.
162 |         cc.endStatement(true);
163 |         break;
164 | 
165 |       case Token.RETURN:
166 |         add("return");
167 |         if (childCount == 1) {
168 |           add(first);
169 |         } else {
170 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
171 |         }
172 |         cc.endStatement();
173 |         break;
174 | 
175 |       case Token.VAR:
176 |         if (first != null) {
177 |           add("var ");
178 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
179 |         }
180 |         break;
181 | 
182 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
183 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
184 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
185 |         break;
186 | 
187 |       case Token.NAME:
188 |         if (first == null || first.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
189 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
190 |         } else {
191 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
192 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
193 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
194 |           if (first.getType() == Token.COMMA) {
195 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN));
196 |           } else {
197 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
198 |             // precedence.
199 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
200 |           }
201 |         }
202 |         break;
203 | 
204 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
205 |         add("[");
206 |         addArrayList(first);
207 |         add("]");
208 |         break;
209 | 
210 |       case Token.LP:
211 |         add("(");
212 |         addList(first);
213 |         add(")");
214 |         break;
215 | 
216 |       case Token.COMMA:
217 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
218 |         addList(first, false, context);
219 |         break;
220 | 
221 |       case Token.NUMBER:
222 |         Preconditions.checkState(
223 |             childCount ==
224 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
225 |               n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) ? 1 : 0));
226 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
227 |         break;
228 | 
229 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
230 |       case Token.VOID:
231 |       case Token.NOT:
232 |       case Token.BITNOT:
233 |       case Token.POS: {
234 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
235 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
236 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
237 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
238 |         break;
239 |       }
240 | 
241 |       case Token.NEG: {
242 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
243 | 
244 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
245 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
246 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
247 |         if (n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NUMBER) {
248 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
249 |         } else {
250 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
251 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
252 |         }
253 | 
254 |         break;
255 |       }
256 | 
257 |       case Token.HOOK: {
258 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
259 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
260 |         addLeftExpr(first, p + 1, context);
261 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
262 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1);
263 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
264 |         addExpr(last, 1);
265 |         break;
266 |       }
267 | 
268 |       case Token.REGEXP:
269 |         if (first.getType() != Token.STRING ||
270 |             last.getType() != Token.STRING) {
271 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
272 |         }
273 | 
274 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
275 | 
276 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
277 |         if (childCount == 2) {
278 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
279 |         } else {
280 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
281 |           add(regexp);
282 |         }
283 |         break;
284 | 
285 |       case Token.GET_REF:
286 |         add(first);
287 |         break;
288 | 
289 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
290 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
291 |         add(first);
292 |         add(".");
293 |         add((String) n.getProp(Node.NAME_PROP));
294 |         break;
295 | 
296 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
297 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
298 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
299 |         }
300 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
301 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
302 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
303 |           add("(");
304 |         }
305 | 
306 |         add("function");
307 |         add(first);
308 | 
309 |         add(first.getNext());
310 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
311 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
312 | 
313 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
314 |           add(")");
315 |         }
316 |         break;
317 | 
318 |       case Token.GET:
319 |       case Token.SET:
320 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT);
321 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
322 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
323 | 
324 |         // Get methods are unnamed
325 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
326 |         if (type == Token.GET) {
327 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
328 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
329 |           add("get ");
330 |         } else {
331 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
332 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
333 |           add("set ");
334 |         }
335 | 
336 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
337 |         String name = n.getString();
338 |         Node fn = first;
339 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
340 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
341 | 
342 |         // Add the property name.
343 |         if (TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
344 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
345 |             // unicode escaped.
346 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
347 |           add(name);
348 |         } else {
349 |           add(jsString(n.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder));
350 |         }
351 | 
352 |         add(parameters);
353 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
354 |         break;
355 | 
356 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
357 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
358 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
359 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
360 |         }
361 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
362 |         if (preserveBlock) {
363 |           cc.beginBlock();
364 |         }
365 | 
366 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
367 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
368 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
369 |                 !preserveBlock &&
370 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
371 |                 n.getParent().getType() == Token.SCRIPT);
372 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
373 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
374 | 
375 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
376 |           if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {
377 |             cc.endStatement();
378 |           }
379 | 
380 |           if (c.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
381 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
382 |           }
383 | 
384 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
385 |           // because top level statements are more homogeneous.
386 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
387 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
388 |           }
389 |         }
390 |         if (preserveBlock) {
391 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
392 |         }
393 |         break;
394 |       }
395 | 
396 |       case Token.FOR:
397 |         if (childCount == 4) {
398 |           add("for(");
399 |           if (first.getType() == Token.VAR) {
400 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
401 |           } else {
402 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
403 |           }
404 |           add(";");
405 |           add(first.getNext());
406 |           add(";");
407 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
408 |           add(")");
409 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
410 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
411 |         } else {
412 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
413 |           add("for(");
414 |           add(first);
415 |           add("in");
416 |           add(first.getNext());
417 |           add(")");
418 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
419 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
420 |         }
421 |         break;
422 | 
423 |       case Token.DO:
424 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
425 |         add("do");
426 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
427 |         add("while(");
428 |         add(last);
429 |         add(")");
430 |         cc.endStatement();
431 |         break;
432 | 
433 |       case Token.WHILE:
434 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
435 |         add("while(");
436 |         add(first);
437 |         add(")");
438 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
439 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
440 |         break;
441 | 
442 |       case Token.EMPTY:
443 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
444 |         break;
445 | 
446 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
447 |         Preconditions.checkState(
448 |             childCount == 2,
449 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
450 |         Preconditions.checkState(
451 |             last.getType() == Token.STRING,
452 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
453 |         boolean needsParens = (first.getType() == Token.NUMBER);
454 |         if (needsParens) {
455 |           add("(");
456 |         }
457 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
458 |         if (needsParens) {
459 |           add(")");
460 |         }
461 |         add(".");
462 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
463 |         break;
464 |       }
465 | 
466 |       case Token.GETELEM:
467 |         Preconditions.checkState(
468 |             childCount == 2,
469 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
470 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
471 |         add("[");
472 |         add(first.getNext());
473 |         add("]");
474 |         break;
475 | 
476 |       case Token.WITH:
477 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
478 |         add("with(");
479 |         add(first);
480 |         add(")");
481 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
482 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
483 |         break;
484 | 
485 |       case Token.INC:
486 |       case Token.DEC: {
487 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
488 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
489 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
490 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
491 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
492 |         if (postProp != 0) {
493 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
494 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
495 |         } else {
496 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
497 |           add(first);
498 |         }
499 |         break;
500 |       }
501 | 
502 |       case Token.CALL:
503 |         // We have two special cases here:
504 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
505 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
506 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
507 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
508 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
509 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
510 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
511 |         // that must be preserved.
512 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
513 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
514 |           add("(0,");
515 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA));
516 |           add(")");
517 |         } else {
518 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
519 |         }
520 |         add("(");
521 |         addList(first.getNext());
522 |         add(")");
523 |         break;
524 | 
525 |       case Token.IF:
526 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
527 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
528 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
529 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
530 |           cc.beginBlock();
531 |         }
532 | 
533 |         add("if(");
534 |         add(first);
535 |         add(")");
536 | 
537 |         if (hasElse) {
538 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
539 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
540 |           add("else");
541 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
542 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
543 |         } else {
544 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
545 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
546 |         }
547 | 
548 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
549 |           cc.endBlock();
550 |         }
551 |         break;
552 | 
553 |       case Token.NULL:
554 |       case Token.THIS:
555 |       case Token.FALSE:
556 |       case Token.TRUE:
557 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
558 |         add(Node.tokenToName(type));
559 |         break;
560 | 
561 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
562 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
563 |         add("continue");
564 |         if (childCount == 1) {
565 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
566 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
567 |           }
568 |           add(" ");
569 |           add(first);
570 |         }
571 |         cc.endStatement();
572 |         break;
573 | 
574 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
575 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
576 |         add("debugger");
577 |         cc.endStatement();
578 |         break;
579 | 
580 |       case Token.BREAK:
581 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
582 |         add("break");
583 |         if (childCount == 1) {
584 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
585 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
586 |           }
587 |           add(" ");
588 |           add(first);
589 |         }
590 |         cc.endStatement();
591 |         break;
592 | 
593 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
594 |         throw new Error("Unexpected EXPR_VOID. Should be EXPR_RESULT.");
595 | 
596 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
597 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
598 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
599 |         cc.endStatement();
600 |         break;
601 | 
602 |       case Token.NEW:
603 |         add("new ");
604 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
605 | 
606 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
607 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
608 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
609 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(first, Token.CALL, new MatchNotFunction())) {
610 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
611 |         }
612 |         addExpr(first, precedence);
613 | 
614 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
615 |         Node next = first.getNext();
616 |         if (next != null) {
617 |           add("(");
618 |           addList(next);
619 |           add(")");
620 |         }
621 |         break;
622 | 
623 |       case Token.STRING:
624 |         if (childCount !=
625 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
626 |               n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) ? 1 : 0)) {
627 |           throw new IllegalStateException(
628 |               "Unexpected String children: " + n.getParent().toStringTree());
629 |         }
630 |         add(jsString(n.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder));
631 |         break;
632 | 
633 |       case Token.DELPROP:
634 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
635 |         add("delete ");
636 |         add(first);
637 |         break;
638 | 
639 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
640 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
641 |         if (needsParens) {
642 |           add("(");
643 |         }
644 |         add("{");
645 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
646 |           if (c != first) {
647 |             cc.listSeparator();
648 |           }
649 | 
650 |           if (c.getType() == Token.GET || c.getType() == Token.SET) {
651 |             add(c);
652 |           } else {
653 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they are
654 |             // not JavaScript keywords
655 |             if (c.getType() == Token.STRING &&
656 |                 !c.isQuotedString() &&
657 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(c.getString()) &&
658 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(c.getString()) &&
659 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
660 |                 // unicode escaped.
661 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(c.getString())) {
662 |               add(c.getString());
663 |             } else {
664 |               addExpr(c, 1);
665 |             }
666 |             add(":");
667 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1);
668 |           }
669 |         }
670 |         add("}");
671 |         if (needsParens) {
672 |           add(")");
673 |         }
674 |         break;
675 |       }
676 | 
677 |       case Token.SWITCH:
678 |         add("switch(");
679 |         add(first);
680 |         add(")");
681 |         cc.beginBlock();
682 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
683 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
684 |         break;
685 | 
686 |       case Token.CASE:
687 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
688 |         add("case ");
689 |         add(first);
690 |         addCaseBody(last);
691 |         break;
692 | 
693 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
694 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
695 |         add("default");
696 |         addCaseBody(first);
697 |         break;
698 | 
699 |       case Token.LABEL:
700 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
701 |         if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
702 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
703 |         }
704 |         add(first);
705 |         add(":");
706 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
707 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
708 |         break;
709 | 
710 |       // This node is auto generated in anonymous functions and should just get
711 |       // ignored for our purposes.
712 |       case Token.SETNAME:
713 |         break;
714 | 
715 |       default:
716 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
717 |     }
718 | 
719 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
720 |   }

===== M0158 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 406-433
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
406 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
407 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
408 | 
409 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
410 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
411 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
412 |       return 0;
413 |     }
414 | 
415 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
416 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
417 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
418 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
419 |       // have been added
420 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
421 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
422 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
423 |       }
424 |     }
425 | 
426 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
427 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
428 |       return 0;
429 |     }
430 | 
431 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
432 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
433 |   }

===== M0159 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.UnionTypeBuilder(JSTypeRegistry)

Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1398 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1399 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false)) {
1400 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1401 |     }
1402 | 
1403 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1404 |     Node n, n2;
1405 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1406 |          res == null && n != null;
1407 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1408 |       if (node2 == null) {
1409 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1410 |       }
1411 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1412 |       if (res != null) {
1413 |         return res;
1414 |       }
1415 |     }
1416 |     return res;
1417 |   }

===== M0177 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 734-776
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
734 |   @Override
735 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
736 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
737 |       return true;
738 |     }
739 | 
740 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
741 |       if (((FunctionType) that).isInterface()) {
742 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
743 |         return true;
744 |       }
745 |       if (this.isInterface()) {
746 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
747 |         return false;
748 |       }
749 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
750 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
751 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
752 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
753 |       // this as an error. It also screws up out standard method
754 |       // for aliasing constructors. Let's punt on all this for now.
755 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
756 |       FunctionType other = (FunctionType) that;
757 |       boolean treatThisTypesAsCovariant =
758 |         // If either one of these is a ctor, skip 'this' checking.
759 |         this.isConstructor() || other.isConstructor() ||
760 | 
761 |         // An interface 'this'-type is non-restrictive.
762 |         // In practical terms, if C implements I, and I has a method m,
763 |         // then any m doesn't necessarily have to C#m's 'this'
764 |         // type doesn't need to match I.
765 |         (other.typeOfThis.getConstructor() != null &&
766 |              other.typeOfThis.getConstructor().isInterface()) ||
767 | 
768 |         // If one of the 'this' types is covariant of the other,
769 |         // then we'll treat them as covariant (see comment above).
770 |         other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
771 |         this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis);
772 |       return treatThisTypesAsCovariant && this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
773 |     }
774 | 
775 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
776 |   }

===== M0188 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.jsString(String,CharsetEncoder)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 907-934
Documentation:
/** Outputs a js string, using the optimal (single/double) quote character */
Method code:
907 |   static String jsString(String s, CharsetEncoder outputCharsetEncoder) {
908 |     int singleq = 0, doubleq = 0;
909 | 
910 |     // could count the quotes and pick the optimal quote character
911 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
912 |       switch (s.charAt(i)) {
913 |         case '"': doubleq++; break;
914 |         case '\'': singleq++; break;
915 |       }
916 |     }
917 | 
918 |     String doublequote, singlequote;
919 |     char quote;
920 |     if (singleq < doubleq) {
921 |       // more double quotes so escape the single quotes
922 |       quote = '\'';
923 |       doublequote = "\"";
924 |       singlequote = "\\\'";
925 |     } else {
926 |       // more single quotes so escape the doubles
927 |       quote = '\"';
928 |       doublequote = "\\\"";
929 |       singlequote = "\'";
930 |     }
931 | 
932 |     return strEscape(s, quote, doublequote, singlequote, "\\\\",
933 |         outputCharsetEncoder);
934 |   }

===== M0201 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 888-915
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
888 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
889 |     // unknown
890 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
891 |       return true;
892 |     }
893 |     // equality
894 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
895 |       return true;
896 |     }
897 |     // all type
898 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
899 |       return true;
900 |     }
901 |     // unions
902 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
903 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
904 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
905 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
906 |           return true;
907 |         }
908 |       }
909 |     }
910 |     // named types
911 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
912 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).getReferencedType());
913 |     }
914 |     return false;
915 |   }

===== M0202 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 417-433
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
417 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
418 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
419 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
420 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
421 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
422 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
423 |         String inputName = input.getName();
424 | 
425 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
426 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
427 |         // will get an error down the line.
428 |         inputs.add(input);
429 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
430 |       }
431 |     }
432 |     return inputs;
433 |   }

===== M0205 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1585-1644
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recurse Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1585 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recurse) {
1586 |     if (type != node.getType()
1587 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1588 |         || getNodeClass(this) != getNodeClass(node)) {
1589 |       return false;
1590 |     }
1591 | 
1592 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1593 |       return false;
1594 |     }
1595 | 
1596 |     if (type == Token.ARRAYLIT) {
1597 |       try {
1598 |         int[] indices1 = (int[]) getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1599 |         int[] indices2 = (int[]) node.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1600 |         if (indices1 == null) {
1601 |           if (indices2 != null) {
1602 |             return false;
1603 |           }
1604 |         } else if (indices2 == null) {
1605 |           return false;
1606 |         } else if (indices1.length != indices2.length) {
1607 |           return false;
1608 |         } else {
1609 |           for (int i = 0; i < indices1.length; i++) {
1610 |             if (indices1[i] != indices2[i]) {
1611 |               return false;
1612 |             }
1613 |           }
1614 |         }
1615 |       } catch (Exception e) {
1616 |         return false;
1617 |       }
1618 |     } else if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1619 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1620 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1621 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1622 |         return false;
1623 |       }
1624 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING) {
1625 |       int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1626 |       int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1627 |       if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1628 |         return false;
1629 |       }
1630 |     }
1631 | 
1632 |     if (recurse) {
1633 |       Node n, n2;
1634 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1635 |            n != null;
1636 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1637 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(n2, compareJsType, true)) {
1638 |           return false;
1639 |         }
1640 |       }
1641 |     }
1642 | 
1643 |     return true;
1644 |   }

/**
   * Detaches child from Node and replaces it with newChild.
   */
Method code:
694 |   public void replaceChild(Node child, Node newChild) {
695 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.next == null,
696 |         "The new child node has siblings.");
697 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.parent == null,
698 |         "The new child node already has a parent.");

704 |     newChild.parent = this;

707 |     } else {

709 |         prev.next = newChild;
710 |     }
711 |     if (child == last)

713 |     child.next = null;

===== M0208 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addNumber(double)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 233-259
Documentation:

Method code:
233 |   void addNumber(double x) {
234 |     // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of x- -4 as
235 |     // x--4 (which is a syntax error).
236 |     char prev = getLastChar();
237 |     if (x < 0 && prev == '-') {
238 |       add(" ");
239 |     }
240 | 
241 |     if ((long) x == x) {
242 |       long value = (long) x;
243 |       long mantissa = value;
244 |       int exp = 0;
245 |       if (Math.abs(x) >= 100) {
246 |         while (mantissa / 10 * Math.pow(10, exp + 1) == value) {
247 |           mantissa /= 10;
248 |           exp++;
249 |         }
250 |       }
251 |       if (exp > 2) {
252 |         add(Long.toString(mantissa) + "E" + Integer.toString(exp));
253 |       } else {
254 |         add(Long.toString(value));
255 |       }
256 |     } else {
257 |       add(String.valueOf(x));
258 |     }
259 |   }

===== M0235 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getNumberValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 253-310
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the value of a node as a Number, or null if it cannot be converted.
   * When it returns a non-null Double, this method effectively emulates the
   * <code>Number()</code> JavaScript cast function.
   */
Method code:
253 |   static Double getNumberValue(Node n) {
254 |     switch (n.getType()) {
255 |       case Token.TRUE:
256 |         return 1.0;
257 | 
258 |       case Token.FALSE:
259 |       case Token.NULL:
260 |         return 0.0;
261 | 
262 |       case Token.NUMBER:
263 |         return n.getDouble();
264 | 
265 |       case Token.VOID:
266 |         if (mayHaveSideEffects(n.getFirstChild())) {
267 |           return null;
268 |         } else {
269 |           return Double.NaN;
270 |         }
271 | 
272 |       case Token.NAME:
273 |         // Check for known constants
274 |         String name = n.getString();
275 |         if (name.equals("undefined")) {
276 |           return Double.NaN;
277 |         }
278 |         if (name.equals("NaN")) {
279 |           return Double.NaN;
280 |         }
281 |         if (name.equals("Infinity")) {
282 |           return Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
283 |         }
284 |         return null;
285 | 
286 |       case Token.NEG:
287 |         if (n.getChildCount() == 1 && n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME
288 |             && n.getFirstChild().getString().equals("Infinity")) {
289 |           return Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
290 |         }
291 |         return null;
292 | 
293 |       case Token.NOT:
294 |         TernaryValue child = getPureBooleanValue(n.getFirstChild());
295 |         if (child != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
296 |           return child.toBoolean(true) ? 0.0 : 1.0; // reversed.
297 |         }
298 |         break;
299 | 
300 |       case Token.STRING:
301 |         return getStringNumberValue(n.getString());
302 | 
303 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
304 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
305 |         String value = getStringValue(n);
306 |         return value != null ? getStringNumberValue(value) : null;
307 |     }
308 | 
309 |     return null;
310 |   }

Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler, String sourceName,
85 |       String sourceStr) {
86 |     try {
87 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceName);
88 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceName, sourceStr,
89 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
90 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
91 |           logger_);
92 |     } catch (IOException e) {
93 |       compiler.report(JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceName));
94 |     }
95 |

97 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
98 |       root = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
99 |     } else {
100 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
101 |     }
102 | 
103 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
104 |     // the source file and module.
105 |     root.putProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP, sourceName);
106 |   }

===== M0277 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 400-415
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
400 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
401 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
402 |       Set<String> directives = null;
403 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
404 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
405 |         if (directives == null) {
406 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
407 |         } else {
408 |           directives.add(directive);
409 |         }
410 |       }
411 | 
412 |       if (directives != null) {
413 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
414 |       }
415 |     }

===== M0286 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1073-1211
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1073 |   Node parseInputs() {
1074 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1075 | 
1076 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1077 |     // individual file parse trees.
1078 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1079 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1080 |     }
1081 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1082 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1083 |     }
1084 | 
1085 |     // Parse main js sources.
1086 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1087 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1088 | 
1089 |     externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1090 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1091 | 
1092 |     externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1093 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1094 | 
1095 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1096 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1097 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1098 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1099 |     }
1100 | 
1101 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1102 | 
1103 |     try {
1104 |       // Parse externs sources.
1105 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1106 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1107 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1108 |           return null;
1109 |         }
1110 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1111 |       }
1112 | 
1113 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1114 |       if (options.manageClosureDependencies) {
1115 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1116 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1117 | 
1118 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1119 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1120 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1121 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1122 |           }
1123 |         }
1124 | 
1125 |         try {
1126 |           inputs =
1127 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1128 |               .manageDependencies(
1129 |                   options.manageClosureDependenciesEntryPoints, inputs);
1130 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1131 |           report(JSError.make(
1132 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1133 |           return null;
1134 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1135 |           report(JSError.make(
1136 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1137 |           return null;
1138 |         }
1139 |       }
1140 | 
1141 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1142 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1143 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1144 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1145 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1146 |           return null;
1147 |         }
1148 | 
1149 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1150 |         if (n == null) {
1151 |           continue;
1152 |         }
1153 | 
1154 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1155 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1156 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1157 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1158 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1159 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1160 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1161 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1162 | 
1163 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1164 | 
1165 |             externs.add(input);
1166 |             staleInputs = true;
1167 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1168 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1169 |             staleInputs = true;
1170 |           }
1171 |         }
1172 |       }
1173 | 
1174 |       if (staleInputs) {
1175 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1176 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1177 |       }
1178 | 
1179 |       // Build the AST.
1180 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1181 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1182 |         if (n == null) {
1183 |           continue;
1184 |         }
1185 | 
1186 |         if (devMode) {
1187 |           runSanityCheck();
1188 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1189 |             return null;
1190 |           }
1191 |         }
1192 | 
1193 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1194 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1195 | 
1196 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1197 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1198 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1199 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1200 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1201 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1202 |         }
1203 | 
1204 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1205 |       }
1206 | 
1207 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1208 |     } finally {
1209 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1210 |     }
1211 |   }

255 |   private void setSourceInfo(Node irNode, AstNode node) {
256 |     // If we have a named function, set the position to that of the name.
257 |     if (irNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
258 |         irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno() != -1) {
259 |       irNode.setLineno(irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno());

/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

===== M0318 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.tryConvertToNumber(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 224-266
Documentation:

Method code:
224 |   private void tryConvertToNumber(Node n) {
225 |     switch (n.getType()) {
226 |       case Token.NUMBER:
227 |         // Nothing to do
228 |         return;
229 |       case Token.AND:
230 |       case Token.OR:
231 |       case Token.COMMA:
232 |         tryConvertToNumber(n.getLastChild());
233 |         return;
234 |       case Token.HOOK:
235 |         tryConvertToNumber(n.getChildAtIndex(1));
236 |         tryConvertToNumber(n.getLastChild());
237 |         return;
238 |       case Token.NAME:
239 |         if (!NodeUtil.isUndefined(n)) {
240 |           return;
241 |         }
242 |         break;
243 |     }
244 | 
245 |     Double result = NodeUtil.getNumberValue(n);
246 |     if (result == null) {
247 |       return;
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     double value = result;
251 | 
252 |     Node replacement;
253 |     if (Double.isNaN(value)) {
254 |       replacement = Node.newString(Token.NAME, "NaN");
255 |     } else if (value == Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY) {
256 |       replacement = Node.newString(Token.NAME, "Infinity");
257 |     } else if (value == Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY) {
258 |       replacement = new Node(Token.NEG, Node.newString(Token.NAME, "Infinity"));
259 |       replacement.copyInformationFromForTree(n);
260 |     } else {
261 |       replacement = Node.newNumber(value);
262 |     }
263 | 
264 |     n.getParent().replaceChild(n, replacement);
265 |     reportCodeChange();
266 |   }
