Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isStrWhiteSpaceChar(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 372-390
Documentation:
/**
   * Copied from Rhino's ScriptRuntime
   */
Method code:
372 |   static TernaryValue isStrWhiteSpaceChar(int c) {
373 |     switch (c) {
374 |       case '\u000B': // <VT>
375 |         return TernaryValue.TRUE;
376 |       case ' ': // <SP>
377 |       case '\n': // <LF>
378 |       case '\r': // <CR>
379 |       case '\t': // <TAB>
380 |       case '\u00A0': // <NBSP>
381 |       case '\u000C': // <FF>
382 |       case '\u2028': // <LS>
383 |       case '\u2029': // <PS>
384 |       case '\uFEFF': // <BOM>
385 |         return TernaryValue.TRUE;
386 |       default:
387 |         return (Character.getType(c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR)
388 |             ? TernaryValue.TRUE : TernaryValue.FALSE;
389 |     }
390 |   }

===== M0330 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 95-156
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
95 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
96 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
97 |     // just bail out early here.
98 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
99 |       return this;
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
103 | 
104 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
105 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
106 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
107 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
108 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
109 |     }
110 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
111 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
112 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
113 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
114 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
115 |         }
116 |       } else {
117 |         if (alternates.size() > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
118 |           return this;
119 |         }
120 | 
121 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
122 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
123 |         // one another.
124 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
125 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
126 |           JSType current = it.next();
127 | 
128 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
129 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
130 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
131 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
132 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
133 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
134 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
135 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
136 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
137 |               return this;
138 |             }
139 |           } else {
140 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
141 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
142 |               return this;
143 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
144 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
145 |               it.remove();
146 |             }
147 |           }
148 |         }
149 |         alternates.add(alternate);
150 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
151 |       }
152 |     } else {
153 |       result = null;
154 |     }
155 |     return this;
156 |   }

===== M0337 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.valueCheck(Node,Predicate<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1173-1188
Documentation:
/**
   * Apply the supplied predicate against the potential
   * all possible result of the expression.
   */
Method code:
1173 |   static boolean valueCheck(Node n, Predicate<Node> p) {
1174 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1175 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
1176 |       case Token.COMMA:
1177 |         return valueCheck(n.getLastChild(), p);
1178 |       case Token.AND:
1179 |       case Token.OR:
1180 |         return valueCheck(n.getFirstChild(), p)
1181 |             && valueCheck(n.getLastChild(), p);
1182 |       case Token.HOOK:
1183 |         return valueCheck(n.getFirstChild().getNext(), p)
1184 |             && valueCheck(n.getLastChild(), p);
1185 |       default:
1186 |         return p.apply(n);
1187 |     }
1188 |   }

===== M0338 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnknownType.UnknownType(JSTypeRegistry,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnknownType.java
Original range: 58-61

===== M0344 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.strEscape(String,char,String,String,String,CharsetEncoder)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 956-1026
Documentation:
/** Helper to escape javascript string as well as regular expression */
Method code:
956 |   static String strEscape(String s, char quote,
957 |                           String doublequoteEscape,
958 |                           String singlequoteEscape,
959 |                           String backslashEscape,
960 |                           CharsetEncoder outputCharsetEncoder) {
961 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(s.length() + 2);
962 |     sb.append(quote);
963 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
964 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
965 |       switch (c) {
966 |         case '\0': sb.append("\\0"); break;
967 |         case '\n': sb.append("\\n"); break;
968 |         case '\r': sb.append("\\r"); break;
969 |         case '\t': sb.append("\\t"); break;
970 |         case '\\': sb.append(backslashEscape); break;
971 |         case '\"': sb.append(doublequoteEscape); break;
972 |         case '\'': sb.append(singlequoteEscape); break;
973 |         case '>':                       // Break --> into --\> or ]]> into ]]\>
974 |           if (i >= 2 &&
975 |               ((s.charAt(i - 1) == '-' && s.charAt(i - 2) == '-') ||
976 |                (s.charAt(i - 1) == ']' && s.charAt(i - 2) == ']'))) {
977 |             sb.append("\\>");
978 |           } else {
979 |             sb.append(c);
980 |           }
981 |           break;
982 |         case '<':
983 |           // Break </script into <\/script
984 |           final String END_SCRIPT = "/script";
985 | 
986 |           // Break <!-- into <\!--
987 |           final String START_COMMENT = "!--";
988 | 
989 |           if (s.regionMatches(true, i + 1, END_SCRIPT, 0,
990 |                               END_SCRIPT.length())) {
991 |             sb.append("<\\");
992 |           } else if (s.regionMatches(false, i + 1, START_COMMENT, 0,
993 |                                      START_COMMENT.length())) {
994 |             sb.append("<\\");
995 |           } else {
996 |             sb.append(c);
997 |           }
998 |           break;
999 |         default:
1000 |           // If we're given an outputCharsetEncoder, then check if the
1001 |           //  character can be represented in this character set.
1002 |           if (outputCharsetEncoder != null) {
1003 |             if (outputCharsetEncoder.canEncode(c)) {
1004 |               sb.append(c);
1005 |             } else {
1006 |               // Unicode-escape the character.
1007 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1008 |             }
1009 |           } else {
1010 |             // No charsetEncoder provided - pass straight latin characters
1011 |             // through, and escape the rest.  Doing the explicit character
1012 |             // check is measurably faster than using the CharsetEncoder.
1013 |             if (c > 0x1f && c <= 0x7f) {
1014 |               sb.append(c);
1015 |             } else {
1016 |               // Other characters can be misinterpreted by some js parsers,
1017 |               // or perhaps mangled by proxies along the way,
1018 |               // so we play it safe and unicode escape them.
1019 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1020 |             }
1021 |           }
1022 |       }
1023 |     }
1024 |     sb.append(quote);
1025 |     return sb.toString();
1026 |   }

===== M0345 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.optimizeSubtree(Node)

69 |   Node optimizeSubtree(Node subtree) {

===== M0356 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.tryFoldBinaryOperator(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 96-163
Documentation:

Method code:
96 |   private Node tryFoldBinaryOperator(Node subtree) {
97 |     Node left = subtree.getFirstChild();
98 | 
99 |     if (left == null) {
100 |       return subtree;
101 |     }
102 | 
103 |     Node right = left.getNext();
104 | 
105 |     if (right == null) {
106 |       return subtree;
107 |     }
108 | 
109 |     // If we've reached here, node is truly a binary operator.
110 |     switch(subtree.getType()) {
111 |       case Token.GETPROP:
112 |         return tryFoldGetProp(subtree, left, right);
113 | 
114 |       case Token.GETELEM:
115 |         return tryFoldGetElem(subtree, left, right);
116 | 
117 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
118 |         return tryFoldInstanceof(subtree, left, right);
119 | 
120 |       case Token.AND:
121 |       case Token.OR:
122 |         return tryFoldAndOr(subtree, left, right);
123 | 
124 |       case Token.LSH:
125 |       case Token.RSH:
126 |       case Token.URSH:
127 |         return tryFoldShift(subtree, left, right);
128 | 
129 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
130 |         return tryFoldAssign(subtree, left, right);
131 | 
132 |       case Token.ADD:
133 |         return tryFoldAdd(subtree, left, right);
134 | 
135 |       case Token.SUB:
136 |       case Token.DIV:
137 |       case Token.MOD:
138 |         return tryFoldArithmeticOp(subtree, left, right);
139 | 
140 |       case Token.MUL:
141 |       case Token.BITAND:
142 |       case Token.BITOR:
143 |       case Token.BITXOR:
144 |         Node result = tryFoldArithmeticOp(subtree, left, right);
145 |         if (result != subtree) {
146 |           return result;
147 |         }
148 |         return tryFoldLeftChildOp(subtree, left, right);
149 | 
150 |       case Token.LT:
151 |       case Token.GT:
152 |       case Token.LE:
153 |       case Token.GE:
154 |       case Token.EQ:
155 |       case Token.NE:
156 |       case Token.SHEQ:
157 |       case Token.SHNE:
158 |         return tryFoldComparison(subtree, left, right);
159 | 
160 |       default:
161 |         return subtree;
162 |     }
163 |   }

===== M0370 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 163-184
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
163 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
164 |     if (isAllType) {
165 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
166 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
167 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
168 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
169 |       } else {
170 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
171 |       }
172 |     } else {
173 |       int size = alternates.size();
174 |       if (size > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
175 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
176 |       } else if (size > 1) {
177 |         return null;
178 |       } else if (size == 1) {
179 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
180 |       } else {
181 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
182 |       }
183 |     }
184 |   }

===== M0380 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(String,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 90-132
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
90 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
91 |                            String sourceString,
92 |                            Config config,
93 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
94 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
95 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
96 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
97 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
98 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
99 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
100 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
101 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
102 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
103 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
104 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
105 | 
106 |     if (config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
107 |       // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
108 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
109 |       compilerEnv.setAllowKeywordAsObjectPropertyName(true);
110 |     }
111 | 
112 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
113 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
114 |     }
115 | 
116 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
117 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
118 |     try {
119 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
120 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
121 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
122 |     } finally {
123 |       Context.exit();
124 |     }
125 |     Node root = null;
126 |     if (astRoot != null) {
127 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
128 |           astRoot, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
129 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
130 |     }
131 |     return root;
132 |   }

===== M0400 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.trimJsWhiteSpace(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 355-367
Documentation:

Method code:
355 |   static String trimJsWhiteSpace(String s) {
356 |     int start = 0;
357 |     int end = s.length();
358 |     while (end > 0
359 |         && isStrWhiteSpaceChar(s.charAt(end - 1)) == TernaryValue.TRUE) {
360 |       end--;
361 |     }
362 |     while (start < end
363 |         && isStrWhiteSpaceChar(s.charAt(start)) == TernaryValue.TRUE) {
364 |       start++;
365 |     }
366 |     return s.substring(start, end);
367 |   }

===== M0403 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 203-231
Documentation:

Method code:
203 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
204 |     maybeEndStatement();
205 | 
206 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
207 |     char prev = getLastChar();
208 | 
209 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
210 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
211 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
212 |       append(" ");
213 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
214 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
215 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
216 |       append(" ");
217 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
218 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
219 |       append(" ");
220 |     }
221 | 
222 |     // Allow formating around the operator.
223 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
224 | 
225 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
226 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
227 |     // because it looks strange.
228 |     if (binOp) {
229 |       maybeCutLine();
230 |     }
231 |   }

66 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
67 |     if (n.getType() == Token.SCRIPT) {
68 |       requiresLineNumbers = false;
69 |     } else if (requiresLineNumbers) {

===== M0429 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processUnaryExpression(UnaryExpression)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 873-899
Documentation:

Method code:
873 |     @Override
874 |     Node processUnaryExpression(UnaryExpression exprNode) {
875 |       int type = transformTokenType(exprNode.getType());
876 |       Node operand = transform(exprNode.getOperand());
877 |       if (type == Token.NEG && operand.getType() == Token.NUMBER) {
878 |         operand.setDouble(-operand.getDouble());
879 |         return operand;
880 |       } else {
881 |         if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
882 |           if (!validAssignmentTarget(operand)) {
883 |             String msg = (type == Token.INC)
884 |                 ? "invalid increment target"
885 |                 : "invalid decrement target";
886 |             errorReporter.error(
887 |               msg,
888 |               sourceName,
889 |               operand.getLineno(), "", 0);
890 |           }
891 |         }
892 | 
893 |         Node node = newNode(type, operand);
894 |         if (exprNode.isPostfix()) {
895 |           node.putBooleanProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP, true);
896 |         }
897 |         return node;
898 |       }
899 |     }

===== M0434 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.tryFoldUnaryOperator(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 325-416
Documentation:

Method code:
325 |   private Node tryFoldUnaryOperator(Node n) {
326 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.hasOneChild());
327 | 
328 |     Node left = n.getFirstChild();
329 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
330 | 
331 |     if (left == null) {
332 |       return n;
333 |     }
334 | 
335 |     TernaryValue leftVal = NodeUtil.getPureBooleanValue(left);
336 |     if (leftVal == TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
337 |       return n;
338 |     }
339 | 
340 |     switch (n.getType()) {
341 |       case Token.NOT:
342 |         // Don't fold !0 and !1 back to false.
343 |         if (left.getType() == Token.NUMBER) {
344 |           double numValue = left.getDouble();
345 |           if (numValue == 0 || numValue == 1) {
346 |             return n;
347 |           }
348 |         }
349 |         int result = leftVal.toBoolean(true) ? Token.FALSE : Token.TRUE;
350 |         Node replacementNode = new Node(result);
351 |         parent.replaceChild(n, replacementNode);
352 |         reportCodeChange();
353 |         return replacementNode;
354 |       case Token.POS:
355 |         if (NodeUtil.isNumericResult(left)) {
356 |           // POS does nothing to numeric values.
357 |           parent.replaceChild(n, left.detachFromParent());
358 |           reportCodeChange();
359 |           return left;
360 |         }
361 |         return n;
362 |       case Token.NEG:
363 |         try {
364 |           if (left.getType() == Token.NAME) {
365 |             if (left.getString().equals("Infinity")) {
366 |               // "-Infinity" is valid and a literal, don't modify it.
367 |               return n;
368 |             } else if (left.getString().equals("NaN")) {
369 |               // "-NaN" is "NaN".
370 |               n.removeChild(left);
371 |               parent.replaceChild(n, left);
372 |               reportCodeChange();
373 |               return left;
374 |             }
375 |           }
376 | 
377 |           double negNum = -left.getDouble();
378 | 
379 |           Node negNumNode = Node.newNumber(negNum);
380 |           parent.replaceChild(n, negNumNode);
381 |           reportCodeChange();
382 |           return negNumNode;
383 |         } catch (UnsupportedOperationException ex) {
384 |           // left is not a number node, so do not replace, but warn the
385 |           // user because they can't be doing anything good
386 |           error(NEGATING_A_NON_NUMBER_ERROR, left);
387 |           return n;
388 |         }
389 |       case Token.BITNOT:
390 |         try {
391 |           double val = left.getDouble();
392 |           if (val >= Integer.MIN_VALUE && val <= Integer.MAX_VALUE) {
393 |             int intVal = (int) val;
394 |             if (intVal == val) {
395 |               Node notIntValNode = Node.newNumber(~intVal);
396 |               parent.replaceChild(n, notIntValNode);
397 |               reportCodeChange();
398 |               return notIntValNode;
399 |             } else {
400 |               error(FRACTIONAL_BITWISE_OPERAND, left);
401 |               return n;
402 |             }
403 |           } else {
404 |             error(BITWISE_OPERAND_OUT_OF_RANGE, left);
405 |             return n;
406 |           }
407 |         } catch (UnsupportedOperationException ex) {
408 |           // left is not a number node, so do not replace, but warn the
409 |           // user because they can't be doing anything good
410 |           error(NEGATING_A_NON_NUMBER_ERROR, left);
411 |           return n;
412 |         }
413 |         default:
414 |           return n;
415 |     }
416 |   }

===== M0442 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 607-619
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
607 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
608 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
609 |     if (typeSet == null) {
610 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
611 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
612 |     }
613 | 
614 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
615 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
616 | 
617 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
618 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
619 |   }

940 |     }
